Title: Aeneae Silvii Senensis de Bohemorum origine ac gestis historia (Historia Bohemica)
Creator: Aeneas Silvius Piccolomini (later Pope Pius II)
Publisher: Coloniae: apud Heronem Alopecium, October 1524 (colophon: "Excusum Coloniae Agrippinae
  apud Heronem Alopecium, Anno M. D. XXIIII, Mense Octobri")
Publication Date: 1524
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Printed in 1524 (colophon); public domain by the project's date rule (published 1930
  or earlier). On Internet Archive, the item metadata carries no rights field; that tag is
  supporting evidence only. Checked 2026-09-30.
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_aeneas-silvius-sene_1524
  (archive.org/details/bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_aeneas-silvius-sene_1524), full-text OCR
  file bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_aeneas-silvius-sene_1524_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl,
  raw bytes) 2026-09-30 by the Library thread; the body below is that file unaltered.
Provenance note: Body unaltered, including its own OCR errors. Header prepended by the Library
  thread. The scan is a microfilm-derived item in the Internet Archive collection labelled
  early-english-books-1641-1700; the label is a collection name and no claim over the 1524 book. It
  is a second printing of the Historia Bohemica beside the vendored 1592 Frankfurt printing.
Content note: A 1524 Cologne printing of the Historia Bohemica, ending with a short life of Aeneas
  Silvius. It prints no marginal notes in the text flow and keeps the long s as ſ in the OCR.
